<div class="col-12 col-lg-7 ml-auto">
<h2 class="font-light headline headline--inverse pb-4 mb-5">About Us</h2>
<p class="my-4 text-justify">iVisa provides the simplest solution for processing your travel visa. Say goodbye to endless research and dealing with foreign governments.   function startKustomerChatDownload(){
    loadjs("https://cdn.kustomerapp.com/cw/sdk.v1.1.min.js");
  }

  function openChatDialog(){
    // Kustomer hasn't been loaded yet
    if(typeof window.Kustomer === "undefined"){
      initKustomerChat(function(){
          Kustomer.describeCustomer({ custom: { chatUrlStr: 'https://www.ivisa.com' } });
          document.getElementById('chat-wrapper').remove();
          Kustomer.open(); // Automatically pop open the chat ASAP
        }
      );

      startKustomerChatDownload();

      document.getElementById('chat-icon').remove();
      document.getElementById('chat-loader').style.display = 'block';
    }
    else {
      if(typeof window.Kustomer.open !== "function"){
        return; // People clicking the icon multiple times while it's still loading
      }

      Kustomer.open();
    }
  }
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">

      function check_homepage_intro(){
        var from = $('#travellingFrom').val();
        var to = $('#travellingTo').find('option:selected').data('url');

        if(from === null || from.length !== 2 || to === null || to === undefined || to.length < 2){
          return;
        }

        window.location.href = to + '?nationality=' + from;
      }

      function init_homepage_intro(){
        $('#travellingFrom').change(check_homepage_intro);
        $('#travellingTo').change(check_homepage_intro);
        $('select.gotoVisaCategoryPage').change(function(){
          window.location.href = $(this).find('option:selected').data('url');
        });
      }

      window.ivisa_js_autorun.push(init_homepage_intro);

    </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    function load_autoscroll(){
      $('#collapseCountries').on('hidden.bs.collapse', function(e){
        var $card = $(this).closest('.most-popular-visas');
        $('html,body').animate({
          scrollTop: $card.offset().top
        }, 800);
      });
    }
    window.ivisa_js_autorun.push(load_autoscroll);
  </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
  loadjs.ready('app', function(){
    // jquery and vue are now loaded!

    $(document).ready(function(){
      // Now the whole DOM is loaded
      for(var i = 0; i < window.ivisa_js_autorun.length; i++){
        var func = window.ivisa_js_autorun[i];
        if(typeof func === "function"){
          func();
        } else {
          alert('Failed to load ivisa_js_autorun');
        }
      }

      $('.btn-toggle').on('click', function(event){
        event.preventDefault();
        var $btn = $(this);
        var $toggle = $btn.data('toggle') || 'active';
        var $target = $btn.data('target') || $btn.attr('href');

        $btn.toggleClass('active');
        $($target).toggleClass($toggle);
      });

    });


  });
</script>
